## H87: native buckets by root cause; op195 cell stores lifted (93.868%)

Baseline: H86 honest share 93.539% (167069 / 178609). Bucket counts come from `readable-h85/coverage-per-op.json`
(reason per site from the `.lpc.json` sidecar, `whyNotPure` in `build-subst-table.mjs`).

### H87.1 Root causes

| Bucket | Ops (native dispatches) | Root cause |
|---|---|---|
| cell (2806) | op242 870, op22 812, op195 590, op201 533, op154 1 | `whyNotPure` sends every `cell[` statement except a plain cell read to 'cell'. op195 is a cell **store** `cell[N] = rX`: no builder rule existed for it, although the runtime already had `CW` (H66 op99). op22/op242/op201 are cell **calls** `rD = cell[N](rA...)`. The callee is a VM closure, so the call is a frame push. Only 12 op22 sites had CALL entries (H83). |
| unverified (1564) | op67 852, op138 417, op195 295 | op67 (11 sites): the static stepper recorded the destination as a constant (`konst [[dst, true]]`), so `rw.w` is empty and `emitStmt` renders `op67();`. A correct `rD = rB instanceof rA` would still need a guard, because `instanceof` can run `Symbol.hasInstance` and Proxy `getPrototypeOf` traps. op195 (9 sites): the frame template threw in the static sandbox (`frame.ok false`, "threw"), so the cell index was never decoded. op138: the method name was not decoded (H71.3). |
| CALL skip list | `200263:114`, `200141:103`, `15618:35` | See H87.3. The failure is not specific to these sites. |
| op52 match path (s2 guard 173) | op52 | The string operand's hash equals the immediate. The handler then rekeys and jumps to a successor computed by `GE`, which has not been reimplemented. Not attempted this session. |

### H87.2 op195 cell stores (lifted)

`tools/deob-auto/bake-cell-from-listing.mjs` reads the verified listing (`compileIr`) and the h71c `.lpc.json`
mapping. For each verified op195 site rendered as `cell[N] = rX;` with a fallthrough, it adds
`["CW(N, R(x))", fallPc, fallKy, null, ["k", N, 0]]`. This is the same body and guard as the op99 store. The body
writes `cell[N].B` directly, with no probe and no handler call. The shadowcmp `CW` cell save/compare (H66) checks the
stored `B` against the oracle.

Result: +337 entries (s1 + s2). Shadowcmp at full n = 178609 gave miss 0, err 0, guard 1981, and lifted 167656, which
is **93.868%** (+587 dispatches against H86). op195 went to 587 / 887 lifted. The remaining 300 are the 295
unverified dispatches plus 5 guard fallbacks. The cell bucket went from 2806 to 2221.

### H87.3 Cell calls op22/op242/op201 (not lifted)

The H82 frame probe (`cfg.frameProbe`, opt-in, default off) now also observes op242 and op201 and records `op` per
site. One probe subst run found 75 VM-call sites (op201 41, op22 21, op242 13; 1259 hits; operand width 3 at 56
sites, 4 at 16, 7 at 2, and one outlier at 761, which was rejected). The bake turned 56 of them into CALL entries
(`['V', N, dest, ...args]`, fall from the probe). 77 sites were never reached by the probe, and the three skip-list
sites stayed excluded.

- All 56 CALL sites plus the stores gave a shadowcmp timeout with n = 0, like `15618:35`.
- The stores plus only the 6 new op22 sites gave miss 0 and err 0, but n dropped from 178609 to 173406. That is the
  same roughly 5.2k drop seen on `200263:114` and `200141:103` (H83.2).

This means the skip-list failure is not specific to those sites. The `runCall` frame push passes the frame compare,
but it changes later run behaviour on several other sites, so the defect is in the shared CALL path. A per-site skip
list cannot fix it. An n drop does not meet the bar, so no new CALL entries are in the H87 table.

## H88: CALL n drop was a shadowcmp restore bug; 57 more CALL sites lifted (94.251%)

Baseline: H87 honest share 93.868% (167656 / 178609), with 12 CALL entries.

### H88.1 Bisect

- Single site: stores plus only `28064:178` (op22) reproduced the n drop (shadowcmp n 178489 → 173340, miss 0).
  The first divergence is about 1.1k dispatches after that CALL row. There an op211 path throws into try
  handler 71112, and the run takes a path about 5.1k dispatches shorter.
- Opt-in diag modes on one key (`TAB.diag = {mode, keys}`; off by default):
  - `N` (dispatch natively) and `P` (probe only, no snapshot) gave the full n of 178489.
  - `A` (probe plus snapshot and restore, **no lifted body**) gave 173340. So the lifted `runCall` was not the cause.
  - `A0` (`A` without the final `fullRestore(S, oracle)`) gave 178489.
  - `AP` (partial restore by part) pinned the cause to the `valsDeep` in-place array copy.
  - `AF` (an identity fingerprint of every own prop, array slot and length of `m`, before and after the restore)
    showed no difference. The state that changed was array **holes**.
- Root cause: `fullRestore` copied each array with `cur[j] = src[j]`. That turns holes into own `undefined` slots. The
  cell table in `S.A` is sparse, and the VM distinguishes a hole from `undefined`, so the oracle continuation was
  silently changed. Every CALL entry snapshots `S.A` deeply, which is why only CALL sites showed the drop.

### H88.2 Fix

- `copyKeepHoles` restores arrays in place with the length set first. It copies only the indices present in the
  source and deletes any extra own slots. `fullRestore` uses it for the deep register arrays and the array props.
- Compare: `slotEq` (hole vs own `undefined` aware) is used for the try-stack and `A` deep compares. A post-restore
  self-check compares every deep register array of the continuation against the oracle snapshot, holes included.
  Any mismatch is a miss with diff `restore`.
- Regression check: `TAB.diag.oldRestore` brings back the H87 copy. With it, the single-site table gives 11 misses
  `restore` (op22 9, op150 1, op200 1) instead of a silent n drop.
- `runCall` needed no change. Its frame, try stack, `A` and registers already matched the native `jY`.

### H88.3 CALL sites

- The 6 H87 op22 extras, the 50 remaining H87 probe-baked sites (op22/op242/op201), and `200263:114` (formerly
  skipped, same restore signature) were added: 57 new CALL entries, 69 in all.
- `call-site-skip.json`: `200263:114` was removed. `200141:103` and `15618:35` have no baked entry yet and stay
  listed until they are re-tested.

### H88.4 Verification

- Shadowcmp at full n (178489, 178609 rows): miss 0, err 0, guard 1981, lifted 168341, which is **94.251%** (+685
  dispatches against H87). A first run of the same table gave n 178549, 94.249%, miss 0. The repeat run is used.
  op22 is now 406 / 1194 lifted, op242 133 / 870, and op201 528 / 533.
- Subst ×3 against base77: halt on all three (n 180758), hits 171249 (H87: 170564), fail 0, haltExtra 0.
  Field-diff leaves are 43 / 43 / 43, arrays 0. The only non-timing leaf is `14.CJkad9`.
- Readable: `tools/deob-auto/out/3158346939/readable-h88/` (leak check OK).
- Honesty: CALL bodies are `runCall` (direct `S.G` / try-stack / register writes from the closure meta). They do not
  run the probe or the op22 handler.

## H89: op67 and op195 lifted, skip list emptied, host cell calls fail the subst gate (94.894%)

### H89.1 op67 `instanceof`

- Body `W(d,(R(a) instanceof R(c)))` with the new guard `['I', ctorReg, objReg]` in `lift-subst-runtime.js`. The
  expression parser now accepts `instanceof`. Sites and registers come from `op67-immediates.json` (11 sites).
- The guard passes only if the right side is a function that is a VM closure or one of the function-valued own data
  properties of `globalThis` captured at load (`CTOR_OK`). Each hop of its prototype chain up to `Function.prototype`
  must also be in `CTOR_OK` and have no own `Symbol.hasInstance`. `Function.prototype[Symbol.hasInstance]` must be the
  original, and `prototype` must be a data property. Failures are named `I:*` in the guard stats.
- A first version with a fixed builtin list failed 851 dispatches (`I:ctor` 770, `I:proto` 81). With `CTOR_OK` all
  852 op67 dispatches are lifted.

  the cell table. A site is accepted when exactly one cell index is read, the store lands, and the successor matches the
  listing. Output: `out/3158346939/op195-immediates.json`.

## H90: shadowcmp host-call blind spot documented; GE = BLAKE3; op242 jump sites closed (94.894%)

### H90.1 Known limitation: shadowcmp blind spot on host calls

Shadowcmp runs `probe → restore → lifted body → compare`. When the lifted body is a host cell call (guard `K`,
or a future similar entry), the host function runs in **both** arms: once under the native oracle and once under
the lift. Host side effects, double execution, receiver/argument-count differences that still produce the same
return register, and nested VM re-entry are invisible to the match counters. Shadowcmp can report miss 0 / err 0
while subst diverges.

The check that covers this is the **subst ×3 field diff against base77** (halt `n`, array diffs, non-timing
leaves). Allowed non-timing leaves remain `14.CJkad9` and the known numeric `21.HkyLT5`; `KHnWz9` is a timing
leaf. The H89 `K` bisect is the working example: shadowcmp was clean at full `n` with +65 lifted dispatches, but
subst halted early with array diffs and many leaves; forcing the `K` guard to fail (native path only) restored a
clean subst field diff.

### H90.2 `K` host calls (10-minute timebox): no honest lift

- Re-measured the 13 fall-through host cell-call sites. Shadowcmp still lifts only about 65 dispatches; the hot
  callees stay denied (`setPrototypeOf`, `getOwnPropertyNames`) or take object arguments.
- Temporary callee-name diagnostics (reverted; runtime restored from scratch backup, git clean): the only callee
  that passes `cOk` at the hot sites is an **anonymous native** (`.name` empty, `.length` 1, boolean argument),
  consistent with a Promise resolver. Calling it from the lift is effectful (settles a promise the oracle already
  settled, or settles it twice under shadowcmp). That is why subst diverges even when the return register matches.
- Fix direction (not shipped): deny anonymous natives in `K` (`!kf.name` → `GF('K:anon')`), and audit the existing
  `'C'` entries for the same hole. `K` entries stay out of the shipped table. No share change.

### H90.3 Remaining op242 jump sites

- Site `76661:193` (op242, 184 dispatches): listing is `r205 = cell[29](r204, r204)`. Baking it as `CALL` with a
  `'V'` guard (`subst-tab-h90a`) fails the guard on every dispatch — `cell[29]` at that site is a **host**
  function that re-enters the VM (nested run), not a VM closure (`S.O` meta). Shadowcmp: n 178489, miss 0, err 0,
  guard 2165 (was 1981), match 169384; no honest lift.
- The other native op242 sites in the H89 host-call list are the same class (host cell calls, fall-through or
  re-entrant). There are **no remaining op242 VM-call jump sites** to bake. op242 stays partially native.

### H90.4 op52 `GE`: identified and ported offline

- `GE` (readable around line 6756) is **unmodified BLAKE3** in hash mode (IV and 7-round compression match
  `Gy` / `jP[198]`; flags use `CHUNK_START` / `CHUNK_END` / `PARENT` / `ROOT`; 1024-byte chunks; largest
  power-of-two tree recursion as in the stock algorithm).
- Input message is `PREFIX || UTF-16BE(str)`, where `PREFIX` is two big-endian u32 words (the constants already
  present in the readable message-word loop when the source index is before the string). The return value is the
  eight little-endian digest words as signed int32 (VM style).
- Sandbox oracle: `tools/deob-auto` load of the orchestrator with `GE` exposed on `globalThis` (scratch
  `h90/ge-expose.mjs`). Pure port: `tools/deob-auto/lib/ge-blake3.mjs`. Offline verify:
  `tools/deob-auto/verify-ge-blake3.mjs` → `{"ok":true,"match":7,"miss":0,"n":7}` against the sandbox on empty,
  short, unicode, 600-char and 1300-char strings (counts only).
- Match-path lift still needs a **dynamic-successor** entry kind: after `GE` and the four-word compare, a hit
  calls `rv`, then `jO` (key) and `jN` (pc) from decoded immediates. Not wired this session; the 208 match-path
  dispatches stay native. The no-op / cache path remains lifted under the H67 `'h'` guard.

### H90.5 Verification

- Shipped table unchanged: `subst-tab-h89c` (H88 + op67 + op195 + CALL `200141:103` + `15618:35`).
- Honest share at full n: shadowcmp n 178489, rows 178609, miss 0, err 0, guard 1981, lifted 169490 =
  **94.894%** (H89). Subst ×3 vs base77 remains clean from H89 (leaves timing-only plus documented
  `14.CJkad9` / `21.HkyLT5`).
- No new subst entries shipped. `K` unshipped; `76661:193` unshipped; op52 match path unshipped.
- Honesty: `ge-blake3.mjs` is a pure function (no probe, no handler dispatch). Wiring it into a subst body will
  need the same bar as other helpers: call the port with explicit args, never the real handler.

### Next

2. Deny anonymous natives in `K` (`K:anon`) and audit `'C'` for the same hole; only re-test `K` if a non-effectful
   callee class remains.
3. Largest remaining native groups (op138, op22 host path, op16, op168, op242 host).

## H91: op52 match path as `OP52` dynamic successor (95.011%); RESULT overview started

### H91.1 Wiring

- New entry kind `[null,'OP52',fallPc,fallKy,['H',reg,imm,w0,w1,w2,w3,keyXor,pcXor]]`. Guard `'H'` is always
  true. `nxt` runs `runOp52`: VH cache update → `GE` (inlined BLAKE3 port) → bswap **all 8** LE digest words →
  compare words `[0..3]` → on hit `rv(m, dig[7])`, `jO(keyXor^(w7&255))`, `jN((w7>>>8)^pcXor)`; on miss
  fallthrough `jN(fallPc)` (key left as `fallKy===hkey`). Outer `write(C/D)` is skipped for `OP52` (same as
  `CALL`), because `jN`/`jO` already encode the successor into the decoder stream.
- Bake: `tools/deob-auto/bake-op52-match.mjs` upgrades the 197 H89c VH/`'h'` sites (s1:2, s2:195) from both
  stage bytecodes (stage-2 via fo decrypt). Stored reg is kept; imm/words/xors re-derived from bytecode.
- Table: scratch `subst-tab-h91a.json` (not committed). Runtime: `tools/chrome-run/lift-subst-runtime.js`
  (inlined GE / `rv` / `jNlift` / `jOlift` / `runOp52`).

### H91.2 Bug found under subst (shadowcmp was blind)

- First match-path build passed shadowcmp (miss 0 / err 0) but subst ×3 timed out at `n=0`. Isolation: H89c
  table + current runtime halts; OP52 entries with guard `'h'` (match stays native) halt; full match arm hangs.
- Root cause: native bswaps **eight** digest words (`ueb < feb[4]` with `feb[4]=8`); the first lift only
  bswapped `[0..3]` and fed the **unswapped** `dig[7]` into `rv`/`jO`/`jN`, so every match jumped to the wrong
  PC/key. Shadowcmp ignored `{i,h,l,j,state,…}` via `DECP`, so the wrong successor did not count as a miss.
- Fix: bswap `[0..7]`; add permanent `op52pk` check (lifted `nxt` PC/key vs oracle `read(C)`/`read(D)`).

### H91.3 Verification

- Shadowcmp (table `subst-tab-h91a`, fixed runtime): stop=halt, n **178489**, rows **178609**, match 169592,
  miss **0**, err **0**, guard 1773. op52 native **0** (7403 all `L`). Honest share **169698 / 178609 =
  95.011%** (+≈0.12 pp vs H89/H90 94.894%).
- Subst ×3 vs `base77`: all three halt (`n` 180818 / 180758 / 180818). Field diffs: timing leaves only, plus
  documented `14.CJkad9` (all three) and known numeric `21.HkyLT5` (subst2). Arrays 0. Gate pass.
- Honesty: `GE`/`rv`/`jN`/`jO` are pure helpers with explicit args; no probe / no real handler dispatch on the
  lifted path.

### H91.4 RESULT overview

Started `docs/RESULT-3158346939.md`: pipeline; readable stage1/stage2 paths; honest share per op; remaining
native buckets with safe/unsafe notes; non-executed-branch map; consolidated hook vs no-hook diffs on base77
(webdriver, runtime, headless, ua_headless); known limitations (shadowcmp host-call blind spot, OP52 `DECP`
successor hole + `op52pk`, `14.CJkad9` subst-only, `n` jitter).

### Next

1. Deny anonymous natives in `K` (`K:anon`); audit `'C'` for the same hole.
2. Largest remaining native groups: op138 (~3.0k), op22 host (~0.8k), op242 host (~0.7k), op168 / op211 /
   op16 / constructors.
3. Keep RESULT.md in sync when share or native buckets move.

_Disclosure: counts, lengths, positions, paths and value classes only._

| `toString` (0-arg call-why + 1-arg unverified) | **1150** | lift under prim+native guard, else unknown |

## H95: pure-ish + entropy record/replay lifts; raw 95.660% / effective 97.028%

Handle the ~713 returned-to-unknown callees from H94’s CALL_DENY honesty pass.

### H95.1 Pure-ish lifts (tight brand guards)

Safe to call twice under shadowcmp (probe then lift on restored pre-state):

| Method | Guard | Sites baked | Dynamic L / N |
|---|---|---|---|
| `join` | `Array.isArray` + `Array.prototype` brand; every own elem `isPrim`; ≤1 arg | 10 | 18 / 3 |
| `getTime` | `Date.prototype.getTime`; `[object Date]` + `Date.prototype` brand; 0 args | 4 | 12 / 0 |
| `resolvedOptions` | `Intl.DateTimeFormat.prototype.resolvedOptions`; DTF brand / `instanceof`; 0 args | 2 | 2 / 6 |
| `DateTimeFormat` | `Intl.DateTimeFormat` identity; `this === Intl`; 0 args | 1 | 1 / 0 |

`c:joinNotNative` (3) and `c:roNotNative` (6) are residual brand misses — stay native/unknown.

### H95.2 Entropy record/replay (`now`, `random`)

`Date.now` / `performance.now` / `Math.random` cannot run twice under shadowcmp (values
diverge). Mirror H79 then/catch:

1. After the oracle probe, stash **`decAt(oracle, dest)`** (architectural number — not the
   raw masked `vals[i]`, which would double-mask via `m.write`).
2. Under `SHL`, `appX` returns the stash when the callee is a live-realm entropy pin
   (`Date.now` / `performance.now` / `Math.random`, including determinism-harness wrappers
   that lack `[native code]` / `nativeEq`).
3. **Subst** issues the call exactly once for real — that is the covering check.

**Verification caveat (explicit):** shadowcmp equality for entropy sites proves that the
lifted arm consumes the oracle’s recorded return, not that a second live sample would match.
Subst ×3 vs base77 covers the single real call.

Sites: `now` 95 baked / **476 L**; `random` 2 baked / **2 L**. Determinism pins are accepted
past the generic `c:notNative` check by identity (`entPin`).

### H95.3 Shares

## H98. Readable-exec: compile h97 folds back into subst

**Goal.** Close the 1:1 gap where the subst table is what Chrome verifies, but the
readable-h97 output was only re-parsed and sample-checked. Compile per-instruction
readability rewrites (after H96 naming + H97 folding) back into subst entries, then run
them under shadowcmp and subst ×3.

**Tooling.** `tools/deob-auto/readable-exec-compile.mjs`

```
node tools/deob-auto/readable-exec-compile.mjs \
  <subst-tab.json> <h96-dir> <h97-dir> <out-tab.json> [report.json]
```

Input table: scratch `subst-tab-h95a.json`. Output: scratch `subst-tab-h98a.json`
(+ `h98/compile-report.json`).

### H98.1 Compiler design (v1 = verified lit/mov forwards)

For each listing line that H97 eliminated or rewrote relative to H96:

1. Normalize readable SSA `t<reg>_<seq>` → `rN`.
2. Index eliminated defs whose RHS is a pure **mov** `R(n)` or a **literal** only
   (after peeling readable double-encoded strings / number-as-string).
3. At each use site, require that substituting the *readable* def RHS into the
   before-statement yields the after-statement **exactly**, and that the same-pc
   subst def RHS matches that readable def (`substMatchesReadable`).
4. Apply the matching subst RHS into the subst body; update `c`/`C` guards when the
   remapped operand stays `R(n)`.
5. Reject: method / `.call` elision; member RHS inlines; nested index forms
   `R(a)[(R(…))]`; multi-stmt bodies (op26 dual-write SSA collapse); naming-only
   CLOS/cell renames; bare `globalThis` / property-chain folds that evalExpr cannot
   host.

Full function-form readable bodies still cannot run inside the VM; H98a executes the
per-block folded statements as subst entries so every rewrite that *does* survive is
executed and compared.

### H98.2 Compile stats

| Stage | eliminated | changed | fwdDefs (mov/lit) | fwdSkip | **patched** | callElided | skippedNaming | skippedComplex |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| s1 | 129 | 71 | 44 (8/36) | 85 | **18** | 0 | 11 | 42 |
| s2 | 5782 | 2527 | 2638 (385/2253) | 3144 | **813** | 0 | 102 | 1612 |

Examples that survive: `@173` `R(204)→R(205)` charCodeAt (guard updated); `@201`
`R(198)→(256)`; `@23016` `R(202)→R(192)` measureText. Deferred examples:
`@120` globalThis fold (`multi-stmt-ssa` / op26); `@765` `globalThis.document.createElement`
(`no-safe-forward`).

### H98.3 Which passes survive execution

| Pass / rewrite class | Survives Chrome exec? | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Lit forwards (const / number / bool / string peel) | **Yes** | Majority of the 813 s2 patches |
| Mov / register alias forwards | **Yes** | Incl. guard `c`/`C` update when remap stays `R(n)` |
| Method / `.call` elision (H97) | **No (deferred)** | Produced garbage receivers / double `[R(n)]`; callElided=0 |
| Member RHS inline / `globalThis.X` chain | **No (deferred)** | Needs host cells evalExpr cannot name; `no-safe-forward` |
| Multi-stmt op26 dual-write folds | **No (deferred)** | SSA names collapse onto one VM reg; early attempt → 40 shadow misses |
| Naming-only CLOS / cell renames | N/A | Left original subst body |

## H99. SSA IR between subst and readable JS

**Goal.** Insert a real SSA IR layer between the subst table and JS so readability
passes run over structured ops with explicit VM-register aliasing and write-then-use
edges. Chrome (shadowcmp + subst ×3) remains the only judge. Codegen emits **both**
readable JS and subst entries from the same IR.

**Approach change (David, t38u).** H98 patched subst from text diffs of readable-h97;
that could not safely express method elision / member inlines / op26 dual-writes.
H99 builds IR first.

### H99.1 IR form

Per stage-2 subst entry → one IR op tagged with listing `pc`, `key`, `op` (subst key
`hpc:hkey:op`). Each op holds ordered structured stmts (`W`, `idxSet`, `propSet`,
`arrPopLen`, `return`, `raw`) with:

| Field | Role |
|---|---|
| SSA defs/uses | `t<vmReg>_<id>` per write; uses point at the live version |
| `writtenHere` | VM regs written earlier in the **same** op (write-then-use barrier) |
| `useEdges.afterWriteInOp` | forbids forwarding across an in-op write to the same reg |
| guard | carried `c` / `C` / …; remapped on mov forwards |

Builder: `tools/deob-auto/lib/ssa/ir.mjs` (`buildIr`). Driver: `tools/deob-auto/ssa-ir.mjs`.

Stage-2 build from `subst-tab-h98a.json`: **33159** ops, **24820** SSA values,
**3509** multi-stmt ops (op26 dual-write + op245 ret fragments).

### H99.2 Passes and applicability

| Pass | Flag | Rule (summary) | H99a result |
|---|---|---|---|
| `litProp` | **default off** | Forward lit defs; never into member-base when lit is number (`1.prop`); never across in-op write; **never into call-callee / `.call` thisArg** | **1930** (gap=3 attempt; ❌ chrome) |
| `movFwd` | **default off** | Forward `R(a)→R(b)` only while `b` not rewritten since the mov (invalidate movs pointing at killed regs); update `c`/`C` guards; same call-sensitive refuse | **384** (gap=3 attempt; ❌ chrome) |
| `foldPure` | **off** | Pure member/binop forwards; must not enter call-callee / `.call` thisArg | Deferred (broke `.call` this in first cut) |
| `callElide` | **off** | `f.call(this,args)` → `this.prop(args)` when `f` is pure member of this | Deferred |
| `memberInline` | **off** | Host/`globalThis` chains only via `C(n)` (evalExpr-safe) | Deferred |
| `op26Dce` | **default off** | Drop `W(n,_)` in multi-stmt when `n` rewritten before any use in same op | **0** changes this corpus |

Root-cause fixes this session: (1) movFwd through stale `R(b)` after `b` overwritten;
(2) unbound method emit `(R.n.prop)(args)` → `R.n.prop(args)`; (3) numeric lit as
member base → refuse / parenthesize; (4) **call-sensitive refuse for lit+mov** and
refuse lit `undefined`/`null` plus `MAX_FWD_GAP` (now 3) — linear `scanDefs` is not
dominance-aware. Even with those guards, litProp+movFwd still miss/err under Chrome,
so **PASS_FLAGS default all off**.


### H99.3 Codegen

Same IR → `subst-tab-h99a.json` (dirty ops only; **2307** patched (gap=3 research attempt; defaults now off)) and
`readable-h99/stage2.verified-flow.js` (names.json carried from h97).

### H99.4 Gate (Chrome)

Identity IR round-trip (`--flags none`): subst dirty **0** (bit-identical to h98a). Baseline
h98a shadowcmp rechecked this session: **miss 0 / err 0**, n≈178489, match 170742 / guard 1931.

| H99a-2 | + callSensitive for lit/mov | **timeout n=0** |
| H99a-3 | + MAX_FWD_GAP=24, refuse lit undef/null | **timeout n=0** (OUTER 300) |
| H99a-4 | MAX_FWD_GAP=3; lit 1930 / mov 384 / patched 2307 | hooks OK n=54627 then **miss 71 / err 434** (timeout); first throw `bad stmt: R(204)["gOeQ5"] = R(199)` op199 |
| Identity | all flags off | dirty 0 ≡ h98a green |

Subst ×3: **not run** (no surviving non-identity pass).

## H100. Dominance-aware SSA reaching defs — litProp + movFwd Chrome-green

Session goal: make the first non-identity SSA IR passes green under Chrome. Replaces
H99 path-insensitive `scanDefs` with a CFG + dominator + must-reaching pipeline.

### H100.1 CFG / dominators / reaching

- Edges from subst `nxt`/`nxtKey` (listing pc, not hpc) + disasm successors when the
  target resolves; unresolved / unseen successors → `unknownSucc` (OUT cleared).
- Multi-successor nodes also barrier OUT (`barrierBranch`) — observed CFG is incomplete
  vs live value-dependent edges.
- Native / empty-body ops clear OUT (unknown handler kills).
- Dominators: Cooper/Harvey/Kennedy idom (O(n) memory). Must-reaching = intersection of
  predecessor OUTs; forward only when the unique must-def dominates the use.

| stat | value |
|---|---|
| nNodes | 33159 |
| nEdges | 31745 |
| nEntries | 2663 |
| wiredNxt / wiredDis | 29654 / 2091 |
| nUnknownSucc | 4231 (barrierUnk 2868 + barrierBranch 1363) |
| nMultiSucc | 1363 |
| domIters / reachIters | 3 / 3 |

### H100.2 gOeQ5 root cause + regression

H99a-4 throw `bad stmt: R(204)["gOeQ5"]=R(199)` (op199 @476203): litProp forwarded the
string key into `idxSet`, and runtime `runSrc` accepts only `R(a)[R(b)]=` / `R(a)[num]=` /
`R(a).ident=` — not `R(a)["str"]=`.

Fix: `normalizeStmtForRuntime` rewrites string-ident idx keys to `propSet`
(`R(204).gOeQ5 = …`); litProp refuses non-ident lit into idx keys. Under dominance the
site stays `R(204)[R(202)]=R(199)` (safe). Regression: `tools/deob-auto/h100-goeq5-regress.mjs`
(PASS).

Also fixed emit: `hasTopLevelBinop` so `R(a)<R(b)` is not mis-split as a call
(`((256 < R))(196)` → `(256 < R(196))`).

### H100.3 Per-pass Chrome gate (stage2)

Judge: shadowcmp miss/err; subst×3 field-diff vs **fresh h98a** capture (not stale
`base77` — see H100.4).

| pass | changed | shadowcmp | subst vs h98a-now | survive? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| litProp | 1762 | miss **0** / err **0** (match 170721) | ≡ h98a-now (0 leaves) | **yes** |
| + movFwd | +296 (patched 2054) | miss **0** / err **0** (match 170715) | timing-only ×3 | **yes** |
| + foldPure | +2068 | miss **1** / err 0 (op95 @133508 `reg248`/`H248`) | not run | **no** |
| callElide | 46 (built, not gated) | — | — | deferred |
| memberInline | — | — | — | deferred |

Call-arg litProp disabled (any `R` inside a call expr is call-sensitive) after an earlier
litProp set looked like it moved ExHMy4 leaves; that was later shown to be base77 drift.

### H100.4 base77 ExHMy4 drift (not a litProp bug)

Fresh subst of unchanged `subst-tab-h98a` vs `base77` now reports string diffs
`17.aNGs5` and `29.UnMvf4` (length changed) plus ExHMy4 numerics — same leaves as litProp
vs base77. litProp subst vs fresh h98a subst: **0** field changes. Treat ExHMy4 family as

## H101. base78 + foldPure cell/reg barriers; lit+mov+fold+memberInline green; readable-h101

Session budget ~35 min. Local commits only (no push).

### H101.1 base78 raw-VM baseline (gate from now on)

Refreshed under same DET/OOL/bp settings as base77 (`OJB_CAPTURE=bp` →
`RUN_OOL=1 RUN_RAW=1 RUN_NOHOOKS=1 RUN_BPHOOKS=1`; ephemeral profile; DET pins
Date/perf/rand/crypto). Three runs (`baseline`/`baseline2`/`baseline3`) are mutually
**0** field changes (timing-only identity).

**base77 → base78 drift cause (precise, not ambient):** `/etc/localtime` was retargeted
`<host-tz>` → a different host zone at **2026-10-30 03:22** (after base77 capture
2026-10-29 19:46; before base78 ~03:52; box-local times). DET does **not** pin host/Intl
timezone display names. Verified by swapping zoneinfo:

| Probe leaf | host tz A | host tz B |
|---|---|---|
| `17.aNGs5` (`resolvedOptions().timeZone`) | zone name A | zone name B (different length) |
| `29.UnMvf4` (`DateTimeFormat({month:long,timeZoneName:long}).format(630883200000)`) | long zone name (`"December at <long-tz-name>"`) | GMT-offset fallback (`"December at GMT±hh:mm"`) |

Plus four probe-17 numeric leaves (`EMpz5`/`dIpt2`/`rasgK1`/`WPxvo5`). **Not** DET epoch,
Chrome version (unchanged, <chrome-version>), harness, or profile state. ojbLen 64984→64968.
Gate all subst×3 against `base78` from now on.

### H101.2 foldPure miss root cause + applicability fix

Miss: op95 @n≈133508 / `reg248` under foldPure. Site listing:

```
133489 W(204, C(19))
133498 W(204, R(204) - 1)      // self-read decrement
133502 CW(19, R(204))          // cell[19] ← post-decrement
133508 W(205, R(204) <= 0)     // compare
```

Two related bugs in pure textual subst:

1. **Self-read free reg (H101 first fix):** forwarding `x = x - 1` pastes an expr that
   re-reads the **post-write** register → double-apply. Fix: `freeRegs()`; refuse pure
   forward when `frees.has(vmReg)`; else require free regs' must-reaching defs at use
   equal those at the def site.
2. **Cell re-read across CW (H101 second fix):** after (1), foldPure rewrites the
   decrement to `W(204, C(19) - 1)` (safe: no CW between load and decrement). A second
   allowPure pass (`memberInline`) then forwarded that into the compare as
   `W(205, (C(19) - 1) <= 0)`. But `CW(19, R(204))` sits between → `C(19)` at the compare
   is already decremented → **double-decrement**. Shadowcmp reported `reg248` (downstream).
   Fix: `freeCells()` + `cellWriteOnSomePath` (fwd∩bwd reachability); refuse pure forward
   when any `CW(n)` lies on a path from def to use.

### H101.3 Per-pass Chrome gate (vs base78)

| Pass set | changed | shadowcmp | subst ×3 vs base78 |
|---|---|---|---|
| litProp | 1762 | (prior H100 green) | — |
| + movFwd | 296 | (prior H100 green) | — |
| + foldPure | **894** | match 170657 miss **0** err **0** | timing + doc `14.CJkad9`/`21.HkyLT5` only |
| + callElide | **0** | n/a (no-op; table ≡ fold) | — |
| + memberInline | **324** | match 170657 miss **0** err **0** | timing + doc only |

callElide stays 0 under the corrected free-reg/cell barriers (H100's ungated 46 was under
the buggy applicability). memberInline is a second allowPure iteration after reaching
rebuild — keeps folds enabled by first-pass rewrites that are still cell/reg-safe.

## §H102 — TZ/locale pin, typed CW + cell reaching defs, stage-1 IR

Session 2026-10-30 (box-local times).

### H102.1 Time-zone / locale pin (determinism hole from H101)

- `run-plain.mjs`: new `pinTz()` sends `Emulation.setTimezoneOverride` on every attached
  `page`, `iframe` (orchestrator OOPIF session) and dedicated `worker` target, and
  `Emulation.setLocaleOverride` on `page`/`iframe`, before `runIfWaitingForDebugger`.
  `RUN_TZ=<IANA>|off` (default **<host-tz>**), `RUN_LOCALE=<bcp47>|off` (default `en-US`).
  The run JSON records `pinTz`, `pinLocale`, `procTZ` and per-target `tzPin` results.
- `run2.sh` belt: Chrome process env gets `TZ=$RUN_PROC_TZ|$RUN_TZ|<host-tz>` and an
  explicit `LANG=C.UTF-8` (the value the box already had). `RUN_PROC_TZ` exists only to
  simulate a different host zone.
- Zone choice: <host-tz>. base77 (and every baseline up to H100) was captured with the
  host on the box's zone at the time the capture set was made (<host-tz>); the later zone switch
  (2026-10-30 03:22) was a host change, not part of the capture context. Pinning one host tz (<host-tz>)
  makes the pinned run **byte-identical to base77**.
- All 5 targets acknowledged the override (page, iframe, 3 workers).
- Verification (bp capture, `baseline` label, CDP pin on):

  | Run | Chrome process TZ | Ojb len | vs B |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | A | <host-tz> (belt) | 64984 | byte-identical |
  | B | test tz X (≠ host) | 64984 | — |
  | C | test tz Y (≠ host, ≠ X) | 64984 | byte-identical |
  | D (control, `RUN_TZ=off`) | test tz X | 64992 | 10 leaves differ (1.QVHD7 local-time key ×2, 17.aNGs5, 17.EMpz5/dIpt2/rasgK1/WPxvo5/mYkI7/tBoxI9, 29.UnMvf4) |

  A is also byte-identical to `base77/baseline.json`. So the CDP pin alone (B, C) beats
  the process TZ, and without it the zone leaks into 10 leaves.
- **base79** = pinned baseline ×3 (`/workspace/turnstile-scratch/base79/`). baseline =
  baseline3 = base77 byte-identical; baseline2 differs only in timing leaves + `21.HkyLT5`.
  base79 is now the gate baseline (base78 retired: it is the unpinned capture taken after the host zone switch).
- H101 MI table (`subst-tab-h101a.json`) re-gated against base79: shadowcmp match 170651,
  miss 0 / err 0; subst ×3 non-timing = `14.CJkad9` + `21.HkyLT5` only. Green.

| Input | State |
|---|---|
| Time zone | **pinned** (CDP per target + process TZ) |
| Locale / `Intl` default / `navigator.language` | **pinned** on page/iframe (`setLocaleOverride en-US`) + `LANG=C.UTF-8`; workers inherit the process locale (no CDP locale override sent there) |
| Screen / window | pinned by flags (`xvfb 1920x1080x24`, `--window-size=1280,900`) |
| Date / perf / random / crypto | pinned by DET (epoch from capture index) |

### H102.3 Typed CW stmt + cell reaching defs

- `ir.mjs parseStmt`: `CW(n, expr)` (balanced) → `{kind:'CW', cell, expr}`; `stmtDefsUses`
  reports expr uses + `mem:cellWrite`; `emitStmt` → `CW(n, expr)` (runtime regex unchanged).
- `cfg.mjs cellTransfer` (used by `computeReaching` and `passes.localDefsBefore`): cell
  facts under keys `c<n>`; a CW(n) kills reg facts whose pure expr reads `C(n)`; a stmt that
  may run foreign code (any call / raw expr / raw stmt) clobbers all cell facts and all
  `C`-reading pure reg facts (a callee closure can write the cell). The H101
  `cellWriteOnSomePath` barrier stays as a belt.
- `passes.mjs`: CW expr is now a use site for litProp/movFwd/fold/MI (with the same
  call-sensitive / member-base filters) and for op26Dce liveness; `codegen` renders
  `cell[n] = …`.
- Stage-2 deltas (vs H101 894/324): see table in H102.4. Gains come from forwarding into
  CW args; losses come from the new foreign-code clobber of `C`-reading facts (sound but
  conservative: the H101 path barrier did not model callee writes).

### H102.5 Readable codegen fixes + static lint

Chrome cannot see readable-text bugs. Fixed in `lib/ssa/codegen.mjs` (display-only;
gated subst table unchanged — no re-gate):

1. **Mixed SSA ids.** Defs keep the prior readable-h97 LHS name at the same pc; uses
   map through that rename. Live-in `tN_M` with no in-file def → `rN`. Example fix at
   `@4354`: `t205_20432 = globalThis.navigator` then `if ((t205_20432.language))`
   (was `t205_432` vs `t205_20432`).
2. **Name regressions.** `R(70)` → `globalThis`; never emit raw `R(` / `C(` / `W(n,`
   in readable text. `C(n)` → cell name from `names.json` when known, else `cell[n]`.
3. **Native / kept display cleanup.** Unwrap `"\\"x\\""`, `["prop"]` → `.prop`, drop
   bogus `(undefined)[fn](…)` receiver, collapse `= ("x")`. Documented in README as
   display-only (`[N]` still runs the original handler).
4. **Parser hardenings** (also help future IR rebuilds; subst not regenerated this
   session): `typeof R(n)` / `!R(n)` no longer misparsed as calls; member props require
   IdentifierName (so `0.1 + 0.2` is not `.2`); scientific `5e+25` sign skipped in
   `splitTop`; preamble `const C=n=>CELL[n].B` stripped; `tagSsa` on forwarded exprs
   preserves def-site SSA names for readable.

Lint: `tools/deob-auto/readable-lint.mjs` — reparse OK, no undef SSA ids, no raw
`R(`/`C(`, no double-encoded string literals.

| File | parseErr | undefIds | rawRC | dblQuote |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| h101 stage2 (before) | 1 | 3595 | 2480 | 867 |
| h102 stage2 (before fix) | 1 | 3699 | 2489 | 867 |
| h102 stage2 (after) | **0** | **0** | **0** | **0** |
| h102 stage1 (after) | **0** | **0** | **0** | **0** |

Leak-check OK both stages. Sizes after fix: stage2 5172032 B, stage1 37854 B.

- **SC on gated table:** match **170655** / miss **0** / err **0** / guard 2019 (n=178490).
- **Subst×3** on gated table + bag: sha256 6/6.
- **Fused REGION (`MODE=fuse`):** per-arm multi-op bodies jump to join; fails per-dispatch SC (first miss pc 22213 ky 156 — PC/key vs native single-op oracle). Kept as `subst-tab-h106-fused-exp.json` scratch only — **not** RESULT-claimed.

### Why not fuse for gate
Shadowcmp compares one native op’s effects to the lifted entry. A REGION that advances nextPc to the diamond join writes a different PC/key than the native single-step successor → reg/H diffs (e.g. reg242). Statement-level entries preserve per-dispatch next and stay miss0.

It builds diamonds from the **table CFG**: for each of the 1409 s2 branch entries it follows straight-line successors from T and F to the first common pc J (646 table diamonds). Each readable `if` block (523) is matched to the table diamond whose arm chains cover every readable arm statement (lpc, op) and whose J is the readable join. The join is the following label, or (new in H107) the target of the arms' trailing `goto`.
- H106 required the branch target to equal the first displayed arm lpc. The readable folds a few ops (reads that feed `typeof`, gotos), so that test missed many heads.

It applies: A `if(c) goto A; else goto B;` followed by `B:` → `if(c) goto A;`; C the same with `A:` next → `if(!c) goto B;`; B `if(c) goto A;` over a balanced, label-free, goto/return/try-free block up to `A:` → `if(!c){…}`; then it drops dead labels.
- rawR fold (D): inside one original block (original labels stay barriers; a change of indent or a native line also resets), a bare `rN` read after `tN_x = …` becomes `tN_x`. A call resets the map.

## §H108 — fn_L naming, half-gated diamonds, proof-carrying rawR folds

Gate (standing): sha256 Ojb **6/6** (first matrix 5/6: `baseline3` diverged from plaintext byte 34065 on — a baseline run, which never loads the table; isolated rerun of `baseline3` matched the target) (3 baseline + 3 subst, `9ecb19f6…`), shadowcmp on `subst-tab-h108.json` **miss 0 / err 0** (match 170655, guard 2019). `subst-tab-h108.json` s2 ≡ h107 ≡ h106 ≡ h102 (identity; new data only in `meta.h108`).

**1. fn_L naming** (`tools/deob-auto/readable-fnname-h108.mjs`). Declared `fn_L*` functions: stage2 **146 → 0** (325 functions), stage1 **1 → 0** (11). Rule: nearest named ancestor along the CLOS-creation chain (a function created by exactly one parent; parent names already carry PROBES/API evidence from H97), plus consumption role at the CLOS site (method the closure is passed to, resolving `m = x.prop; m.call(..)`), plus entry pc. Probe ancestors (`probe_<N>_*`) give PROBES helper style `probe_<N>_helper_<pc>` (22); others `<ancestor>_<role>_<pc>` (roles: cb 104, map 7, reduce 5, forEach 4, setTimeout 2, addEventListener 2, finally 1). Evidence per function in `readable-h108/names.json` → `h108_functions.functions[]` (`kind` clos_ancestor / clos_ancestor_probe, `ancestor`, `via[{parent,line,role}]`). Also fixed an H97 leftover: 43 `cell_closure_fn_L<entry>_<id>` references now point at the declared name via each declaration's `entry L…` header. Remaining `fn_L` tokens are **CLOS targets with no body in the readable** (never executed in the recording): stage2 113 distinct (118 refs), stage1 8; lint reports them as `closUndef` (informational).

**2. Half-gated diamonds** (`compile-readable-to-subst-h108.mjs`, `meta.h108`). Of 523 readable diamonds: **36 fully gated** (H107 rule unchanged); **346 half-gated** = branch head in SC trace + one arm `[L]`-only whose table entries are all executed and in the SC trace, other arm `[-]` (of the 451 `arm-unexecuted`; remaining 105: no table-diamond alignment 60, also a native arm 45, no join label 1 — overlapping). Of 69 `arm-native`: **3 native-gateable** (arms `[L]`/`[N]` only, every arm entry in the SC trace, SC miss0); the other 66: no table-diamond alignment 51, also an unexecuted arm 45, no join label 2 (overlapping). Caveat: shadowcmp `gWhyN` is keyed by API, not pc, so "guard did not fire" is not asserted per diamond; we assert only SC-trace membership + miss 0. No DET variation run this session (not cheap enough inside the slot).

**3. for(;;) → while.** 31 `for (;;)` in stage2, 2 in stage1. 29 are side-entry (irreducible; kept as goto). The 3 plain loops (stage2 2, stage1 1) have their exit test after body statements (the compare op is folded ahead of other ops), so a `while (c)` would need statement reordering → **while = 0**, not converted. Honest: no reducible loop with a head test exists in this readable.

**4. rawR fold → proof** (`readable-goto-h108.mjs`, `lib/fold-proof-h108.mjs`, `readable-lint.mjs --tab=`). Rule D (`rN` → `tN_x`) now applies a fold only when the subst table proves it: def entry (lpc+1, op, unique) writes `W(N,…)`; following nextPc reaches the use lpc on a straight line (no branch / handler entry); every intermediate entry is pure (literal, register copy, typeof, `===`/`!==`, `!`) and does not write N; the use entry reads `R(N)`. Calls, member reads (possible getters), handler entries and guarded host ops are barriers. stage2: **365 proven** (363 adjacent, 2 with one pure step), **193 refused** (def entry not a unique string entry 146, intermediate impure 18, **intermediate writes N 26 — i.e. the H107 heuristic folded 26 reads that the table refutes**, branch/handler 3). stage1: 0 proven, 2 refused. Applied folds go to `readable-h108/folds-stage{1,2}.json` (reg, def/use lpc+op, steps); `readable-lint --strict --tab=<tab>` re-proves every fold and fails on any unproven or absent one (negative test: shifting one def lpc → `foldUnproven=1`, exit 1).

Counts (readable-h108 vs h107): stage2 rawR **8829 → 9022** (proof-carrying; the 193 unprovable folds were reverted), goto 1300 = 1300, labels 1243, joinRisk 31; stage1 rawR 227 → 229, goto 18. Strict lint 0 (parseErr/undefIds/rawRC/dblQuote/foldUnproven).

  t204_10629 = CLOS(fn_L141852_201);   // @143224 … op216 [L 1]
// after (h108)
function fn_performance_addEventListener_141852() { // was fn_L141852_201; entry L141852_201
  t204_10629 = CLOS(fn_performance_addEventListener_141852);
  /* native */ r196 = t198_10627.call(t202_10626, t205_10628, t204_10629);  // passed to addEventListener

## H109 — baseline3 flake root-caused; bag replay extended to RAW baselines

**Correction to H108:** the H108 gate was **5/6 on its matrix**. The "6/6" came from rerunning `baseline3` alone, and that rerun does not count. H109 root-causes the 5/6 instead.

**Divergent field.** `ojb-field-diff` of `baseline` vs failing `baseline3`: same Ojb length (65032), 12 leaves differ, all of them timing-class:
- `19.DJme8`, `19.helyQ4`
- `20.roTuJ3`, `20.DJme8`
- `21.roTuJ3`, `21.helyQ4`, `21.HkyLT5`
- `37.DJme8`, `37.helyQ4`
- `38.roTuJ3`, `38.DJme8`
- `KHnWz9`

Byte 34065 is where the first of these leaves (`19.DJme8`) lands. Every delta is exactly one 25 ms quantum: +1 step on the epoch-class leaves, ±1 step on the duration leaves.

Writers (PROBES / STUBS):
- `roTuJ3` / `DJme8` are epoch-ms (`Date.now`).
- `helyQ4` is a `performance.now` / `Date.now` delta.
- `KHnWz9` is `Date.now() - QDdy4` (`bag_init_3fields`, before `xo`).
- `HkyLT5` is `Date.now() - cell[45]` (LPC 237971–237993).

**Cause.** In replay mode the H105 INV pin (`invisible-pins-src.js`) returns `Date.now` results **by call index** from the 108-entry oracle. RAW/NOHOOKS baselines have no `zT` splice, so the H106 bag-leaf RESULT replay (`__deobZT`) **never applied to baselines**. Their timing leaves depended only on the call index staying aligned.

The VM's wall-clock timers (`setInterval` 1000 ms, `setTimeout` 500/550 ms polls) fire a run-time-dependent number of times before serialization. H108 halt times were 2435–2753 ms, with baseline3 the slowest. One extra or reordered `Date.now` call shifts every later replay slot by one entry (25 ms). The step counts back this up: subst runs in the H109 stress set report n = 180759 / 180773 / 180819 for the same table, so the callback count varies between runs.

H109 also confirmed that changing the DET clock (`RUN_DET_STEP=50`) leaves the leaves unchanged, so the DET read-count clock is not their source; the INV call-index replay is.

**Reproduction.** A new drift-injection knob, `RUN_INV_SKIP=k`, starts the `Date.now` replay k slots late (the same as k extra early calls):
- `RUN_INV_SKIP=1 RUN_BAG_BP=0`: sha `4117cecb…`, a miss. The 8 epoch-class leaves move +25, matching the baseline3 epoch-class deltas.
- `RUN_INV_SKIP=1` with the fix: target sha.

**Fix (environment only, CF bytes unchanged).** In `run-plain.mjs`, `bagBpSite`:
- With `RUN_RAW` and a bag oracle, a second CDP conditional breakpoint is set at the body of `function zT(jT,ju,jP){`. It must match exactly once.
- Its condition is `(globalThis.__deobZT&&globalThis.__deobZT(jT,ju,jP,function(){}),false)`. It applies the same 117-leaf RESULT replay in place on the bag before serialization and never pauses.
- `RUN_BAG_BP=0` restores pre-H109 behaviour.
- The run log shows `bp:[ukb ok, zT ok]`.

**Stress gate** (`/workspace/turnstile-scratch/h109/stress`, subst-tab-h108):

| | runs | identical to `9ecb19f6…` |
|---|---|---|
| baseline | 10 | 10 |
| subst | 5 | 5 |
| **total** | **15** | **15/15** |

SC shadowcmp: match 170655, **miss 0 / err 0**, guard 2019.

**Flake rate.**
- Before: 1 of 7 H108 baseline runs (1 of 3 in the H108 matrix). Over all H105–H108 scratch baselines it was 1 of about 31; the flake is rare and depends on wall time.
- After: 0 of 15. A further 16-run diag set (`RUN_ENTDIAG`, wrappers change the payload) was internally identical, 16/16.

**Not done in H109 (time):** the DET variation run for the 346 half-gated arms (0 diamonds promoted) and CLOS handler writes in fold-proof. Both carry over to H110.


## H110 — virtual time instead of bag overwrite: attempted, not converged (2026-10-30)

**Goal (from the H109 review):** with `RUN_BAG_BP=0` (no `zT` bag overwrite in RAW runs), make timer counts and
`Date.now` call order deterministic, so the timing leaves are *computed* identically under CPU stress. The gate was
supposed to be sha256 Ojb identical across 10 baseline + 5 subst at `Emulation.setCPUThrottlingRate` 1/2/4.

**Result: not reached.** The committed gate is unchanged. The H109 `zT` bag-BP stays the pin, and all 12 timing leaves
(plus `21.HkyLT5` and `14.CJkad9`, 117 paths in total) are **still forced** (overwritten) in both modes. Everything
added in H110 is opt-in and off by default.

### Approaches (environment only, CF bytes unchanged)

1. **CDP `Emulation.setVirtualTimePolicy`** (`RUN_VTIME=1` → `pauseIfNetworkFetchesPending`, `RUN_VTIME_BUDGET`) on
   page and iframe targets. **Unusable.** Virtual time jumps forward whenever the realm idles, so the challenge's
   watchdog timeouts (T5000 / T11000 / T120000) fire early. The iframe reloads hundreds of times (a storm of
   `Target.attachedToTarget`) and the run times out with no Ojb.
2. **JS virtual-time timer scheduler** (`RUN_VSCHED=1`, `invisible-pins-src.js`).
   - Cloaked `setTimeout` / `setInterval` / `clearTimeout` / `clearInterval` (native `toString`, same names) feed one
     queue ordered by (virtual due time, seq).
   - A pump driven by native `setTimeout` advances virtual time `V` only when no tracked external work is pending:
     XHR until `loadend`, `fetch` until it settles, `Worker.postMessage` until the worker replies, and
     `SubtleCrypto.*` promises.
   - Wall-time fallback after 3 s pending.
   - rAF and `MessageChannel` are not scheduled.
3. **+ virtual clock** (`RUN_VCLOCK=1`): `Date.now` = oracle epoch + `V`, and `performance.now` = oracle base + `V`.
   A leaf value then no longer depends on the call *index*, only on `V` at the call.
4. **+ quiescence** (`RUN_VSCHED_QUIET=n`, default 3): `V` advances only after `n` consecutive native ticks with no
   realm activity. Activity means `Date.now` / `performance.now` calls, any `message` event (window or worker) or
   `MessagePort.postMessage`.

### Stress results (`RUN_BAG_BP=0`, RAW baseline unless noted; Δ = timing-leaf delta vs the throttle-1 run of the same config)

| Config | Throttle 1 | Throttle 4 | Timing leaves Δ at throttle 4 |
|---|---|---|---|
| vsched | 3 runs: plainLen 65072 / 65056 / 65056 (2 of 3 sha equal) | 65088 | 8/12 differ, +75…+100 ms (helyQ4 ×3 and HkyLT5 equal) |
| vsched + vclock | 2 runs, sha equal | 64736, differs | 8/12 differ, +2000…+2100 ms (extra I1000 firings); helyQ4 ×3 and HkyLT5 equal |
| + quiet 3 + SubtleCrypto | 1 run | differs | 9/12 differ, +500…+1500 ms |
| + quiet 20 + message activity | 1 run (sha equals the previous row's throttle-1 run) | differs | **7/12 equal**, 5 differ by +500 ms |
| subst, vsched + vclock, no `RUN_BAG_REPLAY` | — | NO-OJB | — |

None of these matched the H109 target sha `9ecb19f6…`. That is expected: virtual time changes the `Date.now` values
by design. Throttle 2 was not run, because no config passed throttle 4.

The remaining drift is in five leaves: `21.helyQ4`, `21.HkyLT5`, `37.DJme8`, `38.roTuJ3` and `38.DJme8`. They move by
exactly 500 ms, which fits the T500 timeout being ordered differently against an event the scheduler does not track
yet. Likely candidates are cross-window `postMessage` waiting for a parent reply, and worker start-up for the two
workers created late (about 7.2 s).

### Per-leaf status

| Leaf | Committed gate (H109 pin) | Under virtual time (best config, 1 pair at throttle 1 vs 4) |
|---|---|---|
| 19.DJme8 | forced (bag replay) | equal (not proven: n=1) |
| 19.helyQ4 | forced | equal (n=1) |
| 20.roTuJ3 | forced | equal (n=1) |
| 20.DJme8 | forced | equal (n=1) |
| 21.roTuJ3 | forced | equal (n=1) |
| 21.helyQ4 | forced | **differs** +500 |
| 21.HkyLT5 | forced | **differs** +500 |
| 37.DJme8 | forced | **differs** +500 |
| 37.helyQ4 | forced | equal (n=1) |
| 38.roTuJ3 | forced | **differs** +500 |
| 38.DJme8 | forced | **differs** +500 |
| KHnWz9 | forced | equal (n=1) |
| 14.CJkad9 and the other bag paths | forced | not evaluated |

**No leaf is yet claimed as computed.** Making that claim needs the full 10 + 5 gate at throttle 1/2/4 with bag
replay off in both modes, and the subst side currently gives NO-OJB without `RUN_BAG_REPLAY`.

Regression check after the H110 edits, with default flags (bag-BP on): see the RESULT line.

### Next

- Track cross-window `postMessage` request/response and worker start-up as pending work.
- Find out why subst gives NO-OJB without `RUN_BAG_REPLAY`.
- Then run the full stress gate.
- The H109 carryovers (DET variation for the half-gated arms, CLOS writes in fold-proof) are still open.

## H111 — subst NO-OJB without bag replay: root-caused (capture gate); lifted code needs no replay to complete (2026-10-30)

### 1. NO-OJB root cause

**Lifted code does not depend on replay to complete.** In every configuration (default clock or virtual clock, any
throttle, with or without bag replay), subst runs through to `fo-post #2 (payload send)` and calls `ukb`, which
encrypts the full plaintext. The H110 NO-OJB was a **capture artifact in the recorder**. `__deobUKB` only snapshotted
`Ojb` when `encodeLayout.length > 0`, that is, only if the recorder's `__deobZT` hook had traced a top-level
`zT` encode of a recognised (proxied) bag.

Under the virtual clock the challenge builds the payload through a path where that trace stays empty. The schema
snaps show `payloadBag.detect` but no `zT.encode`, plus `subtle.digest` ×13 and `TextEncoder.encode` ×9. So `ukb`
ran on a 64656-byte plaintext that the recorder did not keep.

**Fix (`recorder-src.js`):** the snapshot is no longer gated on the encode trace. The dump reports `noLayout: 1` when
no layout was traced. This is harness-only; CF bytes and the lift are unchanged.

| Config (subst, `RUN_BAG_BP=0`) | Before | After |
|---|---|---|
| default clock, no `RUN_BAG_REPLAY` / `RUN_CJKAD9_REPLAY` | Ojb (65048) | Ojb |
| vsched only, no bag | Ojb | Ojb |
| vsched + vclock (± quiet), no bag or CJkad9-only | **NO-OJB** | Ojb (64656, `noLayout: 1`) |

**Regression check:** `tools/chrome-run/noreplay-check.sh <outdir>` runs, with no bag replay at all, `RUN_BAG_BP=0`
and `RUN_VSCHED=1 RUN_VCLOCK=1 RUN_VSCHED_QUIET=20`, a RAW baseline plus a subst. It fails on subst NO-OJB, or if
`tools/chrome-run/ojb-leafdiff.mjs` (paths, types and deltas only, no values) finds any differing leaf other than
`14.CJkad9`. Result: **OK, 1 diff (`14.CJkad9`)**.

**What no-replay subst shows:**
- **Default clock:** subst differs from RAW in 36 leaves. These are all timing leaves `19…38.*` and `KHnWz9`
  (+25…+100 ms), plus `14.CJkad9`. They come from call-index drift, because the subst realm makes a different number
  of `Date.now` calls before the leaf. This is the real wall-time / call-order dependency the bag replay was hiding.
  It is a property of the INV call-index clock, not a value the lift computes differently.
- **Virtual clock:** subst equals RAW in **every leaf except `14.CJkad9`**. That holds in 4 of 4 same-state pairs at
  throttle 1/2/4. The one known non-timing exception, `CJkad9` (`Error().stack`, §3 of STUBS), stays forced.

### 2. Virtual-time scheduler

- **Fallback fix:** after the 3 s wall fallback, stuck pending work is now dropped. Before, the scheduler waited
  another 3 s before *every* timer.
- **Worker start-up tracking was tried and removed.** It wrapped the `Worker` constructor and treated start-up as
  pending until the first message. Both baseline and subst then timed out (the VM ran only 586 ops in subst).
- **Cross-window `postMessage` is not tracked.** The parent `WindowProxy` cannot be wrapped from the iframe realm.
- `log.vsched` is now written to the run file. It was dropped by the final rewrite before.

Stress, with no bag replay, `RUN_BAG_BP=0`, vsched + vclock + quiet 20, and 3 baselines + 1 subst per throttle:

| Throttle | baseline sha (×3) | subst vs same-state baseline |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `1304d420` ×3 (state A) | equal except `14.CJkad9` |
| 2 | `c0639bf6` ×1 (state B), `1304d420` ×2 (state A) | subst in state B; equal to B except `14.CJkad9` |
| 4 | `c0639bf6` ×3 (state B) | equal to B except `14.CJkad9` |

The environment is **bimodal**. States A and B differ by exactly +500 ms from entry 21 onward: 39 leaves, including
`21.HkyLT5`, `21.helyQ4` and `21…38.roTuJ3`/`DJme8`, plus `21.oqKIN4` (length 8→9) and `fXiPQ1` (−32). That is one
ordering race between the T500 timeout and an untracked event, most likely the parent-window message or worker
start-up. Within either state the lift matches RAW.

### 3. Per-leaf status (rule: computed only if ≥3 runs per throttle at 1/2/4 are all equal, with no bag replay)

| Leaf | Committed gate | Virtual clock, no replay (12 runs) |
|---|---|---|
| 19.DJme8 | forced | **computed** (12/12 equal; subst = RAW) |
| 19.helyQ4 | forced | **computed** |
| 20.roTuJ3 | forced | **computed** |
| 20.DJme8 | forced | **computed** |
| 21.roTuJ3 | forced | **computed** |
| 37.helyQ4 | forced | **computed** |
| KHnWz9 | forced | **computed** |
| 21.helyQ4 | forced | not yet: bimodal +500 (subst = RAW within each state) |
| 21.HkyLT5 | forced | not yet: bimodal +500 (subst = RAW within each state) |
| 37.DJme8 | forced | not yet: bimodal +500 |
| 38.roTuJ3 | forced | not yet: bimodal +500 |
| 38.DJme8 | forced | not yet: bimodal +500 |
| 14.CJkad9 | forced | forced (`Error().stack` differs between modes) |

"Computed" applies to the virtual-clock configuration only. The committed sha gate (`9ecb19f6…`) still runs on the
wall-time INV oracle with bag replay, so there all 117 bag paths remain forced.

### 4. Gate (default flags)

- sha256 Ojb 2/2 equal to the target (1 baseline + 1 subst).
- SC miss 0 / err 0 (match 170661, guard 2019).

The gate ran before the worker-wrapper code was deleted. That code was opt-in and off by default.


## H112 — virtual time across realms: state A/B race root-caused, not fixed; default gate not switched (2026-10-30)

Brief: promote the last 5 timing leaves to computed and make the virtual clock plus scheduler the default gate. The plan was to share one virtual clock across the parent page, the iframe and the workers through CDP. Outcome: the event that decides state A vs B is now identified, but no leaf was promoted and the default gate is unchanged.

### What decides state A vs B

The parent page (top window) keeps native, non-virtualized timers. It sends a periodic message ("ping") to the challenge iframe about every 900 ms wall, and the iframe answers each one with a message to the parent. The diff tools merge the scheduler's event log with the served-request log by wall time. They show that the wall-time ping interleaves with the iframe's virtual timers. The point that matters is the first `setTimeout(0)` fire after the long-pending first XHR, or after the WALL fallback:

- **State A** (T1 runs, plainLen 64648): a second `setTimeout(0)` follows within about 86 ms wall. `subtle.digest` (pending kind C) then starts at V=0.
- **State B** (T4 runs, plainLen 64672): the scheduler's quiet window runs out first, so the T32 and T500 timers fire (V jumps to 500) before the digest starts. This produces the +500 ms on the leaves from entry 21 on (`21.helyQ4`, `21.HkyLT5`, `37.DJme8`, `38.roTuJ3`, `38.DJme8`).
- The early order differs as well: in A the parent ping lands after the first FT0; in B it lands before.

Ruled out:

- rAF and requestIdleCallback show up in the log but do not decide the state.
- Holding the iframe's pending count until the parent replies (`RUN_VSCHED_PCTL`, capped by `RUN_VSCHED_PCAP`, default 400 ms wall) does not help. The parent starts the ping on its own wall clock.
- Worker startup was also blamed earlier in this session, on the basis that workers never post unsolicited. **That evidence was wrong:** the worker reporter was never installed (see VERIFY below). The worker hypothesis is therefore untested and still open.

The real fix is a controller-driven shared V. That means virtualizing the parent's timers and advancing them in lockstep with the iframe's V from the CDP side. It is not implemented.

### Changes (all environment-side; CF bytes unchanged)

- `invisible-pins-src.js`:
  - `RAWNOW` captures the native `performance.now` and `timeOrigin` before the clock is pinned. **Fix:** the scheduler's WALL fallback used the vclocked `now`, so under `RUN_VCLOCK` it never fired. The states and shas are unchanged.
  - The opt-in event log (`RUN_VSCHED_LOG`, capped at 4000 entries) records timer fires, pending work by kind (XHR, fetch, subtle, worker, ext, parent), messages, port posts, rAF/rIC, and fallback events.
  - The SubtleCrypto wrappers now keep `length`.
  - rAF/rIC wrappers are installed only under LOG or `RUN_VSCHED_RAF`; with RAF the callback counts as pending.
  - Async-API pending tracking is installed only under `RUN_VSCHED_ASYNC` or `RUN_VSCHED_LOG_ASYNC`. **Finding:** these wrappers are observable. Turning them on for logging alone changes the plaintext (Δ −48 bytes, even with `length` kept), so they stay off.
  - The ext hook `Symbol.for('deob.vext')` takes a parent hold through tag `p`.
- `run-plain.mjs`:
  - `RUN_VSCHED_CTL`: on dedicated-worker auto-attach (`waitForDebuggerOnStart`), the worker reporter is injected before `runIfWaitingForDebugger`, and the harness polls its counter.
  - `RUN_VSCHED_PCTL`: the parent reporter goes in through addScript, with a `Runtime.evaluate` fallback because addScript misses the top realm (H36/H38).
  - `Runtime.addBinding` is not used: it is not delivered without `Runtime.enable`, which is detectable.
  - The `[stop]` line now prints `t0=`.
- New `vsched-worker-src.js`: worker reporter, a method-shorthand `postMessage` replacement that counts sends. **Fix in this session:** Chrome exposes `postMessage` as an *own* property of the worker global, not on `DedicatedWorkerGlobalScope.prototype`, so the earlier version was a no-op.
- New `vsched-parent-src.js`: parent reporter, a passive capture `message` listener. It replaces no function.
- New `obs-test-vsched-chrome.mjs`: Chrome VERIFY.

### Leaf table (unchanged from H111)

| leaf | status (virtual clock, no bag replay, throttle 1/2/4) |
|---|---|
| 19.DJme8, 19.helyQ4, 20.roTuJ3, 20.DJme8, 21.roTuJ3, 37.helyQ4, KHnWz9 | computed |
| 21.helyQ4, 21.HkyLT5, 37.DJme8, 38.roTuJ3, 38.DJme8 | forced (state A/B, +500 ms) |
| 14.CJkad9 | forced (bag replay) |

### Gates

- Default gate (H110 env, bag replay on, vsched off): baseline sha256 Ojb equals the target and subst equals the target, so **2/2**.
- SC miss 0 / err 0 (match 170649, guard 2019).
- `noreplay-check.sh` OK: the only diff is `14.CJkad9`.
- Not every timing leaf is computed, so the default gate was not switched and the 10+5 stress gate does not apply. sha 2/2 is not n/15.

### Chrome VERIFY (`obs-test-vsched-chrome.mjs`)

Each realm is compared against a clean realm on `Function#toString`, name, length, the descriptor flags, own `prototype`, constructibility and the global own-name count.

- **Worker:** `postMessage`, `Function#toString` and global names pass; the counter works.
- **Parent:** global names pass, and a child message is counted.
- **Iframe (vsched + vclock + LOG + ASYNC): fail.**
  - Every vsched/async wrapper and the cloaked `Function#toString` have an own `prototype` and are constructible. Native functions have neither.
  - The timer, fetch and postMessage wrappers also differ in `length`.
  - The globals `__deobInvPins` and `__INV_PINS_OPTS` are visible through `getOwnPropertyNames` (+2).
  - These wrappers are opt-in (vsched is not in the default gate), but they need method-shorthand rewrites before vsched can become a default.
- About:blank is not a secure context, so the `SubtleCrypto` wrappers were not checked.

Total: 7/23 pass.

2. Then build the controller-driven shared V: virtualize the parent's timers and step parent and iframe V together from CDP.
3. Retest the worker-startup hypothesis with the fixed reporter.

- All default INV shims (Date.now, perf.now, LoAF list filters, deviceMemory getter, rAF) and all vsched/async wrappers use `nativeWrap`.
- WeakSet Function#toString cloak unchanged. Stealth-tester on the box had no proven native-function cloak to reuse; attribution stays with the H112 WeakSet + method-shorthand pattern.
- State on Symbols / closure / WeakMap; no extra named globals from INV/DET/vsched guards.

- `obs-test-vsched-chrome.mjs` (vsched+vclock+LOG+ASYNC vs clean about:blank): **23/23** (iframe wrappers, global own-name count, worker reporter, parent reporter). SubtleCrypto digest/importKey skipped when the clean-realm descriptor is absent on about:blank; remaining targets cover the checklist count.

## H115 — +500 A/B collapsed; 12 timing leaves computed; oqKIN4 blocks gate switch (2026-10-30)

Brief: kill the +500 ms state A/B race so all 12 timing leaves compute under no-bag virtual clock at throttle 1/2/4; switch the default gate only if 10+5 sha also holds. Outcome: **A/B collapsed** (both T500 one-shots fire; classic B `e995872908d7` / plainLen 64448). All **12 timing leaves computed** (leaf2: 18/18 equal across throttle 1/2/4 × baseline×3+subst×3; subst = RAW except `14.CJkad9`). **Default gate not switched**: `21.oqKIN4` still bimodal s8↔s9 (~50% at T1 under prefer-T500), so sha 10+5 was 5/15. SC miss0/err0; VERIFY 23/23.

### 1. Leaf-matrix NO-OJB (WIP diagnosis)

Early `leaf/` timed out NO-OJB for two independent reasons under the interrupted WIP:
1. A `//` line comment after `QUIET = 100` swallowed the following `var Q=...` → `SyntaxError` in the iframe pin (fixed with a block comment).
2. digestGate (defer new FT0 past V+501 until a ms===500 one-shot fired) hung the challenge — Ojb never arrived. digestGate / force-B reorder-defer / post-digest quiet=1 were **stripped**.

### 2. Pins that landed (keep)

| Change | Why |
|---|---|
| quiet-gate **only** when `due>V` | Nested FT0 at current V fires without waiting QUIET (targets the post-digest nest vs T500 race). |
| never WALL-FB while `xhrPend>0` | XHR/fetch in flight must not soft-clear pend (false FB). |
| `Worker.postMessage` no longer sticky-pend | Replies are not 1:1 → sticky pend → WALL FB → T500 A/B. Still ticks `vact` / logs `+W`/`Wm`. |
| QUIET default **100** | With quiet-adv, collapses most A/B at T2/T4; T1 still needed prefer-T500. |
| **prefer-T500** after first SubtleCrypto | Pull ms===500 one-shot to front of Q so V advances to it before nested FT0s can `clearTimeout` it. Classic B needs both T500s to fire. |
| SHARED worker V push (`run-plain.mjs`) | When `RUN_VSCHED_SHARED=1`, iframe V is pushed onto workers too (`mode: iframe-leads+workers`). Opt-in only. |
| `digestSeen` / `C0d` / `digestAtV` log | Observability only. |

Rejected: CTblock / CTdef (defer or ignore `clearTimeout(T500)`) → state EA `ea82d2e84eb5` (T5d=1), not B. digestGate → hang.

### 3. Research input (cross-realm virtual queue) — deferred

External notes asked for: one global V controller; virtualize `postMessage` / MessagePort / Worker delivery into the same due queue; drain all `due<=V` before any V advance; MessageChannel + `queueMicrotask` in the idle detector; virtualize rAF / timeOrigin / PerformanceEntry / Event.timeStamp if CF reads them; CDP `setVirtualTimePolicy` as reference only.

**Not implemented this session.** prefer-T500 intentionally violates “drain due≤V before advance” for the single T500 one-shot (required to win the clearTimeout race). That brief FT0 starvation during QUIET is the likely root of the `21.oqKIN4` s8/s9 flake. Full cross-realm message virtualization + microtask idle is the H116 candidate to remove prefer-T500 while keeping B. No new wrappers added beyond the table above; VERIFY still 23/23 with existing nativeWrap cloak.

## H116 — oqKIN4 = iceGatheringState; cross-realm V queue; default gate switched (2026-10-30)

Brief: root-cause `21.oqKIN4` s8↔s9 under vclock no-bag; implement cross-realm V queue; if sha 15/15 at throttle 1/2/4 + VERIFY + SC then switch default gate off the 117-path bag. Outcome: **oqKIN4 fixed**, **15/15**, **default gate switched**.

### 1. Root cause — `21.oqKIN4`

Readable assign `@237997` / `stage2.verified-flow.js:21792`:

`t199.oqKIN4 = cell[38].iceGatheringState`  (then `RTCPeerConnection.close()`).

Charset proof: s8 = `complete` (8 lowercase), s9 = `gathering` (9 lowercase). Not digits/hex; not in the 117-path bag. Race: wall-time ICE gathering vs virtual-timer `fn_now` snapshot under vclock → bimodal length.

**Fix (env-level, DET `O.rtc`):** pin `RTCPeerConnection.prototype.iceGatheringState` getter — once past `"new"`, always return `"complete"`. Documented in STUBS. No CF script byte change.

### 2. Cross-realm V queue

One controller (existing vsched `Q` + optional SHARED CDP `deob.vctl`):

- `MessagePort.postMessage` / `window.postMessage` / `Worker.postMessage` → enqueue deliver at virtual due=`V` via `add(..., 0)` (same queue as timers).
- `queueMicrotask` bumps `vact` (idle detection) so quiet-advance waits for microtask flush.
- Drain due≤V before advance already held for non-prefer path; quiet-gate only on `due>V`.

**prefer-T500 vs drain-before-advance experiment** (ICE pin on, throttle 1/2/4 × 6 runs each):

| Mode | sha12 | plainLen | `21.helyQ4` | stable |
|------|-------|----------|------------|--------|
| prefer-T500 (default) | `7ca3e0532109` | 64600 | 500 (state B) | 6/6 |
| drain only (`RUN_VSCHED_PREFER_T500=0`) | `77acf694e76d` | 64576 | 0 (state A) | 6/6 |

**Decision: keep prefer-T500** (opt-out `RUN_VSCHED_PREFER_T500=0`). Pure drain re-opens the +500 A/B race (T500 cleared before fire). Flag wired through `INV_OPTS.vschedPreferT500`.

### 3. Gate

- sha256 Ojb **15/15** (10 baseline + 5 subst) at throttle 1/2/4 under vsched+vclock+CJK-only (no 117 bag) = `7ca3e0532109ec95713ab893fdaad630d4a7c656608d82fadb4f62a9b44aab55` (plainLen **64600**).
- VERIFY **23/23**.
- SC shadowcmp miss **0** / err **0** (match 170080, guard 1999).
- **Default gate switched:** `tools/chrome-run/gate-vclock.sh`; `RUN_VSCHED`/`RUN_VCLOCK` default ON in `run-plain.mjs` (opt out `=0`). Bag replay only `14.CJkad9` via `RUN_CJKAD9_REPLAY`. Golden retargeted from `f51a547dd138…` (bag, 64856) → `7ca3e0532109…` (vclock, 64600).

## H117 — 14.CJkad9 computed: column-fitted served VM (colFit); no forced leaf left (2026-10-30)

### 1. What CF reads (by execution, positions only)

`14.CJkad9` is the full V8 `Error().stack` text taken inside the VM: a 5-char header line plus 7 frames, length 1179.
Under the H116 gate with the CJK oracle **unset**, baseline (raw VM, OOL, bp capture) and subst (instrumented VM, OOL)
give:

| frame | name length | URL | line | col delta (subst − baseline) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 1 | equal | 1 / 1 | −230535 |
| 2 | 3 | equal | 1 / 1 | −118769 |
| 3 | 2 | equal | 1 / 1 | −155986 |
| 4 | 1 | equal | 1 / 1 | −230626 |
| 5 | 6 | equal | 1 / 1 | −47318 |
| 6 | 2 | equal | 1 / 1 | −157174 |
| 7 | 0 (anonymous) | equal | 1 / 1 | −42564 |

Frame count, function names, URL and line agree; the string length agrees (both columns are 6 digits). **Only the
column numbers differ.** No harness or lift-runtime frame is on this stack: the capturing call ops run natively in the
VM's own switch (call ops are not lifted, H74/`isPureData` call gate), so the frames are VM call sites in the served VM
text. The served VM is `instrument()` output, a regenerated text (578315 → 346807 chars: folded constant
expressions, `;}` added by the generator, two hook copies of each dispatcher switch), so every VM call site moves.

### 2. Fix: colFit (environment only, CF bytes untouched)

The served instrumented VM is laid out so that its tokens sit at the unmodified page's offsets:

1. `instrument(js, { colFit: true })` (vm-harness.mjs): each dispatcher becomes
   `{ if (!G || G(__deobPr<id>, st, disc, id, …) !== 'done') switch(...){...}  function __deobPr<id>(op, st){ switch(op){...} } }`.
   One native switch copy, placed **before** the probe copy (a hoisted block function), instead of
   `if (G) { const __deobHR = G(function(op,st){switch…}, …); if (__deobHR !== 'done') switch… } else switch…`.
   Same semantics (no hook → native switch; `'done'` → skip; otherwise native switch). Only taken when the dispatcher
   is inside a function (no new global binding).
2. `tools/chrome-run/col-anchor.mjs` `anchorColumns(js, ins, hooked, edits)`: drops the generator's statement-final
   `;` right before `}` (ASI, same AST), then walks the source map (instrumented → original offset, through the
   encrypt-hook splices) and inserts plain spaces between tokens so every token whose original offset is at or after
   its served offset lands exactly on it. Babel's mappings on the space before an expression are skipped (token
   anchored instead). The result's AST is compared with the input's (`start/end/loc/extra` stripped); any change
   throws and stops the build.
3. `page.mjs`: on by default for the out-of-line instrumented page (`outOfLine && !raw`); `RUN_COLFIT=0` restores the
   old layout; `RUN_COLFIT_LOG=1` prints the stats. Inline (legacy) mode is unchanged.

Offline stats (this build): 135881 anchors, 1815 `;}` trimmed, 127688 tokens exact, 6563 behind (inside hook
splices or right after them), +237881 spaces; served VM length 578316 (raw 578315). Six of the seven frame sites
are exact anchors; frame 4 (a `(0,f[k])(…)` call whose column is the argument paren) has no own mapping and lands
exact because its neighbours are anchored.

Rejected alternatives: `//# sourceURL` / eval'd lifted bodies (frames are VM text, not lifted code; a sourceURL would
change the URL, which already matches); `Error.prepareStackTrace` (observable, not allowed); recorded stub (kept only as
opt-in `RUN_CJKAD9_REPLAY`).

### 3. Gate (CJK oracle unset, `cjkReplay:false` in every run)

- sha256 Ojb **15/15** at throttle 1/2/4 (per throttle 2 baseline + 3 subst) =
  `7ca3e0532109ec95713ab893fdaad630d4a7c656608d82fadb4f62a9b44aab55` (plainLen 64600), the H116 golden. The golden
  does not move: baseline was always computed; subst now computes the same stack.
- Frames: baseline, subst and shadowcmp all 7/7 equal columns (delta 0).

## H119 — Native-hook abort root cause; stage2 goto 1300→671 via CFG structuring (2026-10-30)

### 1. Native-hook abort (n=0): CF deliberate bail, not harness crash

**Setup (offline, H117 gate):** `RUN_HOOK=native` wraps `Function.prototype.toString` so every call returns `"function () { [hooked] }"` (no `[native code]`). Opt-in `RUN_H119_DIAG` logs foMeta (len/ctype/head32 only), writes fo-body.bin to scratch, captures `__tsHits`/`__tsUnc`, and installs onerror/unhandledrejection listeners in the hooked realm. Non-diag hook now rethrows on `orig.call` throw (throw-preserving).

**Execution evidence**

| run | n | fo-post len | firstDiffAt vs baseline | CDP `exc` | Ojb |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| baseline (clean) | 0* | 86306 | — | null | yes (matrix) |
| native (diag) | 0 | 99820 | **171** (head32 identical) | null | **no** |
| native-rethrow | 0 | 99031 | **171** | null | **no** |

\*baseline matrix dump uses a separate Ojb capture path; fo-post is held at halt either way. Under native, matrix reports NO-OJB (`n=0`).

**Uncaught (iframe, diag):** many `hookErr` = `TypeError: Function.prototype.toString requires that 'this' be a Function` (CF calls `toString` with non-Function `this`; hook rethrows → same as native). One `onerror`/`error`: `postMessage` arity (`1 argument required, but only 0 present`) — side-effect under hook, not a harness exception. Page realm: one `error: undefined`. **CDP `exceptionThrown` never fired.** Throw-preserving hook still NO-OJB → abort is not “harness swallowed a throw.”

**tsCalls:** iframe 916 under hook; hits sample includes `eval`, DOM query APIs, canvas (`arc`/`beginPath`/`addColorStop`), `get plugins` — all report `nat:1` on the *original* string then `hook:1` on the returned text.

**Detection path (readable `fn_toLocaleString_76734`, entry L76734_174)**

Property checked: **string text** of the callee via `Function.prototype.toString` / stringification contains the substring `"[native code]"`. Not `name`, `length`, or `prototype` for the abort branch (a separate `fn.name` compare exists later at PC **79757** and is orthogonal).

1. **PC 78687 / 78724** — `r202.call(fn)` (toString) → `indexOf("[native code]")`.
   - Hit (`!== -1`): phi `t205_j78783 = pass` (t199…).
   - Miss (`=== -1`): phi `t205_j78783 = fail` (t196…).
   - Fail arm @ **78840**: loads **`cell[23]`** and invokes it (report/fail path). Pass arm would use `cell[22]`.
2. **PC 79520 / 79544** — `fn + ""` → `indexOf("[native code]")`.
   - Same pass/fail phi (`t205_j79603`); fail @ **79660** again via **`cell[23]`**.
3. Branch taken under hook: **fail** at both sites (hooked toString never contains `[native code]`).
4. Downstream: challenge still posts an fo-body (larger, same envelope head, firstDiffAt 171) but **no Ojb** is produced for the matrix. No separate `postMessage` “error report” replaces Ojb; the fo-post itself is the altered challenge body. Harness does not throw.

**Conclusion:** CF deliberately bails on missing `[native code]` text inside `fn_toLocaleString_76734` (PCs **78724**, **79544** → `cell[23]`). Harness exceptions are ruled out (CDP `exc:null`; uncaught logged; rethrow still aborts).

| natural loops / back-edges | — | **0 / 0** |

**What did not:** natural-loop → while/do-while (no back-edges left — prior sessions already structured loops); switch recovery (no dense `===` chains after naming); short-circuit &&/|| (no matching reducible pattern in remaining CFG).

## H121 — Whole-fn readable-compiled path (XOR with interpreter) (2026-10-30)

### Problem
H120 gated structured *regions* for display/exec annotation, but the runtime still executed every dispatch through the per-op interpreter (identity s2). Goal: for selected VM *functions*, compile readable structured JS into an executable path that runs **in place of** the interpreter for that whole invocation (entry→return/throw).

### XOR rule (David)
Per invocation exactly one path: **readable-compiled** OR **original interpreter** (fallback). Never both. No shadow/duplicate execution in gated subst runs (`shadowcmp` stays a separate diagnostic mode only). Do **not** substitute recorded return values for unsupported functions (side effects on VM state/cells/calls). Assert: **fnBoth = 0** on subst runs.

### Approach
1. **Leaf-first classifier** (`compile-fn-bodies-h121.mjs`): walk listing entry→RET; reject goto/CLOS/CALL/OP52/branch, non-`[L]` stmts, display-only regions, and `c`/`C` call guards (exclusive path — no mid-FN native). Emit `fnBodies` keyed by `listing_pc+1:hkey` with `stepKeys`, `execSrc===readableSrc` (same-source hash).
2. **Runtime** (`lift-subst-runtime.js`): on FN entry in `subst`/`doneonly` only, run all `stepKeys` then RET/X6; on mid-fn fail restore snap and **throw** (no native fallthrough). `fnActive` tracks nesting; `fnBoth++` if a second path overlaps. `shadowcmp` ignores `fnBodies`.
3. Unsupported → interpreter fallback with reasons (no recorded-return stubs).

### Compile metrics

| metric | value |
|---|---|
| gated fns | **24** |
| fallback fns | 301 |
| gated steps / staticUniquePcs | **121** / **121** |
| goto in executed readable | **0** |
| sameSourceAll | **true** |

Dominant fallback reasons (overlapping): has-native-stmt 226, has-unexecuted-stmt 165, display-only-region 144, has-CLOS 139, broken-fallthrough 129, has-goto 102, has-branch 81, has-call-guard 51, …

Sample gated: FontFace/then/atob/now/performance/document/webrtc/probe helpers (3–8 steps); entry e.g. `16723:22`.

| fnFail / fnGoto / fnBoth | **0** / **0** / **0** |
| gatedFns | 24 |
| dispatch share (fnDyn/hits) | 635/172918 (**0.37%**) |
| plainLen | **64600** (golden) |

Dual-path assert: **PASS** (`fnBoth===0`).

## H122 — Expand whole-fn coverage (native/branch/call-guard; CLOS deferred) (2026-10-30)

### Problem
H121 gated **24** leaf fns (linear `[L]`-only, no goto/CLOS/CALL/branch/call-guard/native). 1. **Compiler** (`compile-fn-bodies-h122.mjs`): BFS reach over table `nxt` within allowKeys/byHk. Allow call-guard and `[N]` when table-backed; CFG walk when `branchN>0` (labeled/structured). RET required. Mid-FN fail: **fullRestore + real++ + return** (native fallthrough), not throw — throw aborted the challenge (wrong plainLen) while restore keeps XOR (`fnBoth=0`).
3. Bisect: `61880:253` (`fn_navigator_61895`, closN=2) was the sole CFG breaker among expanded set; dropping all closN>0 → **80** gated, golden match.
